## 2026-11-01T08:12:53+00:00 — EQC/ITT draft wording correction

- Clarified past JV experience versus present participation.
- Completed the balance-sheet exception/older-year condition.
- Removed unsupported immediate-rejection timing.
- Qualified the blanket ITT clarification conclusion.
- Claim count unchanged; updated IDs and prior wording retained below and in claim editorial history.
- Sources/schema/checkpoints unchanged. No API, Git operation or backup. Human review pending.

```json
[
  {
    "page": "concepts/eligibility_cum_qualification_criteria.md",
    "previous_id": "Cfb3368d0db5455c0",
    "new_id": "C288911257527c4d4",
    "previous_text": "For similar nature works executed as a member of a Joint Venture (JV), the value of work is recognized strictly in proportion to the tenderer's stipulated financial share in that JV, and a copy of the JV Agreement must be provided.",
    "corrected_text": "For previously completed or substantially completed similar nature work performed as a JV member, the recognised work value is proportional to the tenderer’s stipulated financial share in that JV, and a copy of the JV Agreement must accompany the bid. This past-experience provision must be read separately from EQC’s single-entity-only participation rule for this tender."
  },
  {
    "page": "concepts/eligibility_cum_qualification_criteria.md",
    "previous_id": "Cb134bf41ea94d889",
    "new_id": "C8a9ac5a7208c318d",
    "previous_text": "If the audited balance sheet for the financial year ending 2025-2026 is not available, the tenderer must submit an affidavit certifying that the balance sheet has not been audited so far, in which case financial data from the preceding four audited financial years will be evaluated.",
    "corrected_text": "If the audited balance sheet for the financial year ending 2025–2026 is unavailable, the source requires an affidavit certifying that it has actually not been audited so far; in that case, financial data from the previous four audited financial years is considered. The same note states that failure to submit an audited balance sheet for any other year, even when requested through clarification under ITT 20, results in the tender being considered non-responsive. This records the stated rule, not a decision on any particular bid."
  },
  {
    "page": "contractual_obligation_map.md",
    "previous_id": "C370e5e8589ecbd7f",
    "new_id": "Cfc914468f59e7ea9",
    "previous_text": "False declarations regarding Class-I local supplier status result in immediate proposal rejection and debarment for up to two years.",
    "corrected_text": "EQC 2.9 states that a bidder’s false declaration of Class-I Local Supplier status results in rejection of its proposal and debarment for up to two years."
  },
  {
    "page": "contractual_obligation_map.md",
    "previous_id": "C7fa05fe487688ed0",
    "new_id": "Cf039177347bd7ac0",
    "previous_text": "Mandatory eligibility and qualification documents must be fully submitted before the established deadline, as subsequent clarifications cannot cure initial omissions of mandatory credentials.",
    "corrected_text": "ITT 20.2 requires documents establishing eligibility and qualification, and other mandatory requirements, to be submitted before the deadline. Under ITT 20.1, the Employer may request clarification but is not obliged to do so; additional documents unrelated to the clarification sought are not permitted, and responses leading to material changes are not considered. These provisions do not by themselves support a blanket statement that every initial omission is incapable of clarification."
  }
]
```

    "new_id": "C2a712608ef4265c0",
    "previous_text": "Works in hand information declared under Attachment-7 of the Letter of Technical Bid must be certified either by the statutory Independent Financial Auditor under the Companies Act or by a qualified Chartered Accountant.",
    "corrected_text": "Works-in-Hand information in Attachment 7 to the Letter of Technical Bid must be certified either by the company’s Independent Financial Auditor (Statutory Auditor) appointed under the Companies Act, or by a Chartered Accountant with the CA’s stamp and signature, Membership Number and Firm Registration Number."

    "corrected_text": "EQC financial-standing Note 7 states that, for its evaluation convention, a January–December calendar year corresponds to the subsequent April–March fiscal year; its example maps January–December 2023 to FY 2023–24 (April 2023–March 2024), with consistent application to such conversions.   }
]

            "json_record_id": null,
            "source_review_status": "pending_manual_review",
            "quote": "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"12\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_12\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\\n\\n\\n      FORM OF BANK GUARANTEE FOR TENDER SECURITY/ EARNEST MONEY DEPOSIT\\n\\n                       (To be stamped in accordance with the Stamp Act of the Country of Issuing Bank)\\n\\n     To,\\n    Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\\n      3rd Floor BMTC Complex,\\n     K.H. Road, Shanthinagar\\n     Bengaluru- 560027.\\n     Karnataka, INDIA.\\n\\n1.  WHEREAS ______________ [Name and Address of the Tenderer] (hereinafter referred to as the “Bidder” or\\n     “Tenderer”) has  undertaken  for  submission  of  Tender  in  pursuance  of  Tender  Notification  No.\\n    _____________ for _________________________ [Name of the Works] (hereinafter referred to as the “Bid”\\n     or “Tender”).\\n\\n2.  AND WHEREAS it has been stipulated in the Tender issued by Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\n    (BMRCL) (hereinafter referred to as the “Employer” or “BMRCL”) that the Tenderer shall furnish to\\n    Employer an unconditional and irrevocable Bank Guarantee from an Indian Scheduled Bank (excluding\\n     Cooperative Bank) or from a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined in Section 2(e) of RBI Act 1934 read with\\n    second schedule, for the sum specified herein below as Tender Security(EMD) for compliance of the\\n     obligations in accordance with the Tender conditions. In pursuance to this, M/s_________ has requested\\n      for issuance of the Bank Guarantee for the sum specified herein below as Tender security (EMD).\\n\\n\\n3.  AND WHEREAS we _____________________________ [Name and full address of the Bank including Email\\n     address] having registered office at ____________________________ (hereinafter referred to as the\\n     “Bank”), which expression shall unless repugnant to the context or the meaning thereof, include  its\\n     successors, administrators, executors, assignees, do hereby agree to give the Employer such a Bank\\n    Guarantee drawn and payable at Bengaluru ________ branch on behalf of the tenderer.\\n\\n\\n4.  NOW therefore, the Bank hereby affirm that  it is the guarantor and responsible to ‘Employer’ and their\\n     successors,  on  behalf   of  the  ‘Tenderer’  up  to  a   total  sum   of  INR/Foreign  Currency/ies\\n    _________________________________ [the amount of Guarantee to be specified in figure and words in the\\n     respective currency/ies as  specified  in  the Tender document]. Further, the Bank hereby unconditionally,\\n     irrevocably and without demur undertake to immediately pay, the Employer upon the Employer’s  first\\n     written demand and without cavil, or arguments any sum or sums within the limits of the sum specified in\\n      this Bank Guarantee as aforesaid without the Employer being required to prove or to show grounds or\\n    reasons for the demand, by depositing/crediting to the Current Account of the Employer, the sums\\n     specified herein.\\n\\n\\n5.   This Bank Guarantee is encashable at our designated Branch as mentioned below:\\n\\n    Name of the Bank, Branch and contact details Address Telephone no: & Fax No: E-mail Address Branch Manager/s\\n    name & mobile No: Bank Zonal Office Address, Telephone No, Fax Number, E-mail Address: __________________\\n                                   OR\\n     This e- Bank Guarantee shall be encashable at the issuing branch of the Bank through NeSL portal.\\n6.  The Bank hereby confirms that the Bank is on the SFMS (Structural Finance Messaging System) platform\\n    and shall invariably send an advice of the Bank Guarantee to the designated Bank of the Employer as\\n     given below:\\n\\n         Sl. No.  Particulars              Details\\n                i      Name of Beneficiary     Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\n                ii      Name of Bank         Bank of India\\n                iii       Branch               Cantonment Branch, Bengaluru.\\n          iv       Address of Branch       49, Jyothi Mahal,\\n                                                St. Mark’s Road, Bangalore - 560001\\n       v       Account No.           840420110000760\\n          vi      IFSC Code:           BKID0008404\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"7.   This Bank Guarantee shall be encashed at the demand of the Employer on or before the due date.\\n\\n8.  The Employer shall have full rights to encash this bank guarantee at any time during the validity period and\\n     the ‘Tenderer’ shall have no right or claim whatsoever in the matter of encashment of the Bank guarantee\\n    amount by the ‘Employer’. The Bank will be fully responsible in terms of the guarantee and obligation, to\\n   make immediate payment to ‘Employer’, without the consent of the Tenderer and without referring the\\n     matter to the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n9.  The Bank will deliver the money required by the ‘Employer’ immediately on demand without delay without\\n     reference to the ‘Tenderer’ and without the necessity of a previous notice or of judicial or administrative\\n    procedures and without it being necessary to prove to the Bank the liability or damages resulting from any\\n     defects or shortcomings or debts of the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n10.  Courts at Bengaluru, Karnataka, India shall have exclusive jurisdiction for adjudicating the disputes arising\\n     out of the encashment of the Guarantee, and, the said Bank undertakes not to revoke this Guarantee\\n     during its currency, except with the previous consent of the ‘Employer’ in writing and agree that any change\\n      in the constitution of the Bank shall not discharge its liability hereunder.\\n\\n11.  This Bank Guarantee will not be discharged due to the change in the constitution of the ‘Tenderer’ or\\n    change in the constitution of the bank.\\n\\n12.  This Deed of guarantee is signed and executed by Shri/Smt ______________ and Shri/Smt ______ who\\n     is/are authorized by the Bank for issuing the guarantee on  its behalf and their ID No/s. and Email\\n    address/es being________ & ________ and ________ & ________ respectively.\\n\\n13. The Bank’s liability under this Guarantee shall not exceed the amount of ______ [Indian Rupees/Foreign\\n     Currency/ies to be specified in words and figures].\\n\\n14.  This guarantee shall be valid till _________ [Date to be mentioned].\\n\\n15. The pendency of any dispute or legal proceedings shall not affect this guarantee in any manner.\\n\\n16.   It is hereby agreed that the liability of the Bank under this guarantee shall cease on whichever of the\\n     following events first occurs.\\n        a) Payment by the Bank of the guaranteed sum in full to the ‘Employer’,\\n                                                 or\\n        b) Receipt of written communication from the ‘Employer’ to the Bank along with the   original Bank\\n        Guarantee discharging the Bank of  its  liability covered by this Bank Guarantee and in case of\\n          electronic Bank Guarantee (e-BG), after release/discharge of e-BG by the Employer through NeSL\\n           portal.\\n\\n                            SIGNATURE AND SEAL OF THE GUARANTOR\\n\\n                               DATE: ___________________\\n                         NAME OF THE BANK: ______________________________\\n                            ADDRESS: _______________________________________\\nNote:\\n1.  The stamp paper of appropriate value shall be purchased in the name of the bank, who issues the “Bank\\n    Guarantee”.\\n2.  The above format shall be used for e-Bank Guarantee also.\\n3.   For issuance of e-BG, the UIN (PAN No.) of BMRCL is AAACB4881D.\\n4.  Tender No. as mentioned in relevant para of ITT pertaining to Tender Security shall be the reference to be\\n    mentioned in the e-BG.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\",\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"source_marking\": \"not_marked_deleted\"\n}",

          {
            "id": "S19382a03427e15ba",
            "okf_path": "OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ.md",
            "okf_sha256": "9439a74058830fd65e3d20892e276927f56b1c5717d424ea357f19aca5698795",
            "heading": "1.0 — Pricing Document Preamble and Instructions",
            "source_document": "Vol4DLNP1.pdf",
            "pdf_pages": [
              6
            ],
            "quote": "- **Read BOQ with the Complete Tender and Contract Documents** (Instruction 1)\n- **Provisional Quantities and Payment for Measured Work** (Instruction 2)\n- **Price Bid Preparation in the Prescribed XLS Sheet** (Instruction 3; ITT 10)\n  - Schedule-A: percentage rate to two decimals; At Par / Excess / Less\n  - Schedule-B: At Par only\n  - Schedule-C: lump-sum salvage credit to BMRCL\n- **Proportionate Recovery and Adjustment of Salvage Credit** (Instruction 4)\n- **Salvage Credit Based on Quoted Price** (Instruction 5)\n- **Taxes and Statutory Levies Included, Except GST** (Instruction 6)\n- **Measurement of Completed Work** (Instruction 7)\n- **Completed-Work Pricing and Included Execution Costs** (Instruction 8)\n- **Included Safety and Protection Costs** (Instruction 9)\n- **Overheads, Profit, Risks and Defect-Remedy Obligations** (Instruction 10)\n- **Accommodation, Insurance, Wages and Approval Costs** (Instruction 11)\n\n\n\n- **Execution Standards and Engineer’s Directions** (Instruction 12)\n- **Incidental Removal Works Without Separate Payment** (Instruction 13)\n- **Temporary Relocation and Protection of Utilities** (Instruction 14)\n- **Temporary Works, Power and Supports at Contractor’s Cost** (Instruction 15)\n- **Permanent Supports and Restoration Payment Provisions** (Instruction 16)\n- **Balcony and Canopy Area at 50%; Chejjas Excluded from Payment** (Instruction 17)\n- **Demolition Depth and Refilling of Low-Level Areas** (Instruction 18)\n- **Removal Within the Line of Cut, Excluding Trees** (Instruction 19)\n- **Total Floor Area as the Sum of Built Areas of All Floors** (Instruction 20)\n- **Building Additions or Deletions and Accepted Percentage Rates** (Instruction 21)",
            "page_basis": "OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified",
            "page_precision": "section_reference"
          },
          {
            "id": "S469ca02217533c37",
            "okf_path": "OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ.md",

            "pdf_pages": [
              8
            ],
            "quote": "- **Schedule-A — Demolition and Dismantling**\n  - Partial & Full demolition and dismantling work as per details given in Schedule-A of the BOQ\n  - **Estimated Cost:** INR 4,69,41,306.00\n\n- **Schedule-B — KPWD Schedule-of-Rate Items**\n  - Items under Schedule of Rate published by KPWD\n  - **Estimated Cost:** INR 5,00,000.00\n\n- **Total of Schedules A+B:** INR 4,74,41,306.00\n\n- **Schedule-C — Salvage Credit to BMRCL**\n  - Credit for salvageable items from dismantled/demolished private properties in which the owner has no interest\n  - **Source Reference:** Para 15 of Part-3 of Employer’s Requirement\n  - **Estimated Cost Field:** `----`\n  - **Pricing Basis:** Lump-sum credit under Preamble Instruction 3",
            "page_basis": "OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified",
            "page_precision": "section_reference"

September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.             "source_review_status": "pending_manual_review",
            "quote": "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.           {
            "id": "S19382a03427e15ba",
            "okf_path": "OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ.md",
            "okf_sha256": "9439a74058830fd65e3d20892e276927f56b1c5717d424ea357f19aca5698795",
            "heading": "1.0 — Pricing Document Preamble and Instructions",
            "source_document": "Vol4DLNP1.pdf",
            "pdf_pages": [
              6
            ],
            "quote": "- **Read BOQ with the Complete Tender and Contract Documents** (Instruction 1)\n- **Provisional Quantities and Payment for Measured Work** (Instruction 2)\n- **Price Bid Preparation in the Prescribed XLS Sheet** (Instruction 3; ITT 10)\n  - Schedule-A: percentage rate to two decimals; At Par / Excess / Less\n  - Schedule-B: At Par only\n  - Schedule-C: lump-sum salvage credit to BMRCL\n- **Proportionate Recovery and Adjustment of Salvage Credit** (Instruction 4)\n- **Salvage Credit Based on Quoted Price** (Instruction 5)\n- **Taxes and Statutory Levies Included, Except GST** (Instruction 6)\n- **Measurement of Completed Work** (Instruction 7)\n- **Completed-Work Pricing and Included Execution Costs** (Instruction 8)\n- **Included Safety and Protection Costs** (Instruction 9)\n- **Overheads, Profit, Risks and Defect-Remedy Obligations** (Instruction 10)\n- **Accommodation, Insurance, Wages and Approval Costs** (Instruction 11)\n\n\n\n- **Execution Standards and Engineer’s Directions** (Instruction 12)\n- **Incidental Removal Works Without Separate Payment** (Instruction 13)\n- **Temporary Relocation and Protection of Utilities** (Instruction 14)\n- **Temporary Works, Power and Supports at Contractor’s Cost** (Instruction 15)\n- **Permanent Supports and Restoration Payment Provisions** (Instruction 16)\n- **Balcony and Canopy Area at 50%; Chejjas Excluded from Payment** (Instruction 17)\n- **Demolition Depth and Refilling of Low-Level Areas** (Instruction 18)\n- **Removal Within the Line of Cut, Excluding Trees** (Instruction 19)\n- **Total Floor Area as the Sum of Built Areas of All Floors** (Instruction 20)\n- **Building Additions or Deletions and Accepted Percentage Rates** (Instruction 21)",
            "page_basis": "OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified",
            "page_precision": "section_reference"
          }
        ],
        "created_at": "2026-10-31T15:57:39+00:00",
        "status": "draft"
      },

            "quote": "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n        476.6429138183594,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        514.2384033203125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n        523.5628662109375,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        602.1983642578125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n        611.5228271484375,\n        526.3880615234375,\n        649.1182861328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n        658.4427490234375,\n        526.3875732421875,\n        709.71826171875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99981689453125,\n        719.042724609375,\n        526.3865966796875,\n        756.6382446289062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"305\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}",
            "page_basis": "Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified",

following contract terms and expressions as used in the contract defined shall\\n                             have the meanings assigned to them, except where the context otherwise requires.\\n                           Words indicating persons or parties include corporations and other legal entities\\n                                except where the context requires otherwise.\\n\\n                                to be submitted by the Contractor.\\n                        1.1.1.3     “Contract” means the written Contract Agreement, the Letter of Acceptance,\\n agreement pursuant to acceptance\\n                                           letter  referred  to  in Sub-Clause  1.4.   It  shall  also  include  all subsequent\\n                                    modifications/  amendments  to  record  the  Contract  as  a  result  of  the\\n                               communications or negotiation proceedings between the parties.\\n                        1.1.1.5     “Contractor’s Proposal” means the proposal submitted by the Contractor with the\\n                                Tender, as modified and accepted by the Employer and included in the Contract.\\n                            Such documents may include the Contractor’s preliminary Design.\\n                        1.1.1.6    “Design Data” means all specifications, plans, drawings, details, graphs, sketches,\\n                                models, levels, setting-out dimensions, calculations duly checked by the Contractor\\n                             and other documents relating to the Design of the works prepared or to be prepared\\n                              by or on behalf of the Contractor.\\n                        1.1.1.7    “Drawings” means the Employer’s Drawings and the Drawings submitted by the\\n                                  Contractor and any modification of such drawings as any, from time to time, be\\n                                   furnished or for which the Engineer has issued a Notice of No Objection.\\n                        1.1.1.8    “Employer’s               applicable) as included in the Contract, and any alterations and modifications\\n                                    thereto in accordance with the Contract.\\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"1.1.2      Persons\\n\\n                 1.1.2.1     “Party” means the Employer or the Contractor as the context requires.\\n                 1.1.2.2     “Contractor” shall mean the person, firm, association of firms, Company or\\n                            Corporation, Joint Venture identified as the Contractor and whose tender for the\\n                        work has been accepted by the Employer and responsible for carrying out the\\n                            construction scope, in accordance with the Contract Documents and unless the\\n                           context otherwise so requires shall include his/their executors, administrators,\\n                         successors and permitted assignees.\\n                 1.1.2.3     “Contractor’s Representative” means the person (if any) named as such in the\\n                           Contract or other person appointed from time to time by the Contractor under Sub\\n                        Clause 4.3.\\n                 1.1.2.4    \\\"Designated Contractors\\\" means any of the following whose activities or the\\n                        works they are engaged to carry out, affect or are affected by the Works, in any way\\n                             or at any time:\\n                                  (a) Contractors, design consultants and utility authorities engaged on the Project\\n                               from time to time by the Employer;\\n                                 (b) Sub-contractors of any  tier of the contractors above; provided that the\\n                                      definition shall exclude the Contractor and his sub-contractors of any tier in\\n                                     relation to the Works.\\n                 1.1.2.5    “Designer” means the Contractor, or part of the group forming the contractor,\\n                           person, firm or company or group of companies, or any replacement, carrying out\\n                           the Design or part thereof.\\n                 1.1.2.6    “Employer” means BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n                       (BMRCL), its legal successors and assignees.\\n                 1.1.2.7    “Engineer” means Chief Engineer or Addl. Chief Engineer or Deputy Chief\\n                         Engineer or General Consultant or any person/party nominated or appointed from\\n                           time to time by the Employer to act as the Engineer for the purposes of the Contract\\n                       and notified as such in writing to the Contractor.\\n                 1.1.2.8    “General Consultant” or “GC” means the firm/JV or consortium of firms appointed\\n                        by the Employer as a general consultant to the Employer or any other person\\n                          appointed from time to time and notified as such to the Contractor.\\n                 1.1.2.9     “Sub-Contractor” means  the  Individual,  Firm, Company,  Corporation,  Joint\\n                         Venture or Consortium, having direct contract with the Contractor and to whom any\\n                             part of the Work has been sublet by the Contractor, with prior permission of the\\n                         Engineer or Employer and shall include his heirs, his executors, administrators,\\n                          successors, legal representatives, as the case may be.\\n                1.1.2.10   “Partner/member” means partner or member of  joint venture, as the context\\n                             requires.\\n                1.1.2.11    “Tenderer” or “Bidder” means the person, firm or corporation, Joint Venture\\n                            submitting a tender against the notice of invitation of tender and shall include his\\n                               heirs, his executors, administrators, successors, legal representatives, as the case\\n                    may be. Tenderer shall mean ‘Contractor’ wherever the context so requires and\\n                             vice-versa.\\n                1.1.3      Dates, Times and Periods\\n\\n                 1.1.3.1    “Commencement Date  “will be reckoned as the date of signing of Contract\\n                        Agreement.\\n                 1.1.3.2     “Contract Period” means the period from the Commencement Date to the end of\\n                                  final Defects Liability Period.\\n                 1.1.3.3    “Day” means a calendar day; “Week” means 7 calendar days; “Month” means\\n                         Georgian calendar month and “Year” means 365 calendar days.\\n                 1.1.3.4     “Gazetted Holiday” means every holiday which is observed by Bangalore Metro\\n                             Rail Corporation Limited as a gazetted holiday.\\n                 1.1.3.5    “General Holiday” means Sunday.\\n                 1.1.3.6    “Key Date  / Mile Stones” means a date identified as such in the Contract\\n                       documents as the same may be extended by the Engineer pursuant to GCC clause\\n                              8.4.\\n                 1.1.3.7    “Stage” means level of progress of the works identified as such and more\\n                               particularly described in the Employer’s Requirements for which a Key Date for the\\n                        achievement thereof is stipulated in the Contract.\\n                 1.1.3.8    “Time for Completion” means the time for completing the Works physically or a\\n                            section or a part thereof (as the case may be), and passing the Tests on Completion\\n                             including  Integrated  Testing  and Commissioning, as stated in the Contract and\\n                        as applicable for the nature of contract calculated from the Commencement Date.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"1.1.4      Tests and subsystem,  system,  etc.  during  and/or  after\\n                               manufacture in the factory.\\n                        1.1.4.2    \\\"Integrated  Testing\\\"   In the contracts where applicable means the programme\\n                                      of tests performed by the Contractor at the direction of the Engineer.\\n                        1.1.4.3    “Key date certificate” means the certificate to be issued by the Engineer in relation\\n                                      to the achievement or otherwise of Key dates.\\n                        1.1.4.4    “Performance Certificate” means the certificate issued by the Engineer under\\n                              Sub-Clause 10.9.\\n                        1.1.4.5    “Taking over Certificate,” means a in the Contract and designated as such, and any other such tests as may\\n                             be agreed by the Engineer and the Contractor, or instructed as a Variation, which\\n                                 are to be carried out before the Works, or any Section are taken over by the\\n                                Employer.\\n\\n                        1.1.5     Money and Payments\\n\\n                        1.1.5.1     “Contract Price “or “Contract Value” means the sum stated in the Letter of\\n                              Acceptance as payable to the Contractor for execution of the work including\\n                               remedying of any defect during completion, defect liability period therein, subject to\\n                              such additions thereto or deductions there from as may be made under the\\n                                   provisions of the Contract.\\n                        1.1.5.2    “Tender Price” is the quoted price at the time of bidding in the Financial Proposal.\\n                        1.1.5.3    “Cost” means  all expenditure properly incurred  (or  to be  incurred) by the\\n                                   Contractor, whether on or off the Site, including overhead and similar charges, but\\n                             does not include profit.\\n                        1.1.5.4     “Final Payment Certificate” means the payment certificate issued by the Engineer\\n                               under Sub-Clause 11.11.\\n                        1.1.5.5     “Final Statement” means the agreed statement defined in Sub-Clause 11.9.\\n                        1.1.5.6     “Foreign Currency” means a freely convertible international trading currency in\\n                               which part of the Contract Price is payable, but not the Local Currency.\\n                        1.1.5.7     “Interim Payment Certificate” means any payment certificate issued by the\\n                                Engineer under Sub-Clause 11.5, other than the Final Payment Certificate.\\n                        1.1.5.8     Local Currency” means Indian Rupees (INR).\\n                        1.1.5.9     “Retention Money” is the Security Deposit for the performance of the obligations\\n                                      of the contractor during the contract period.\\n                       1.1.5.10   “Tender Security/Earnest Money” means a tender guarantee in the form of an e-\\n                            Bank Guarantee /Insurance Surety Bond to be furnished along with tender by the\\n                                 Tenderer.\\n\\n                       1.1.5.11   “Performance Security” means a guarantee for satisfactory completion of the work\\n                              by the contractor as per Sub clause 4.2.1 of GCC and the Employer is guaranteed\\n                               compensation for any monetary loss up to the amount of performance guarantee.\\n                        1.1.6      Other Definitions\\n                        1.1.6.1     “Contractor’s Equipment” means all machinery, apparatus, appliances, other\\n                                    things of whatsoever nature required for purpose of the Contract, including without\\n                                         limitation, Contractor’s plant and equipment, or materials to or from the Site, but\\n                             does not include Plant, or Materials intended to form or forming part of the\\n                             Permanent Works.\\n                        1.1.6.2     “Materials” means things of  all kinds (other than Plant) to be provided and\\n                                  incorporated in the Permanent Works by the Contractor, including the supply-only\\n                                 items  (if any), which are to be supplied by the Contractor as specified in the\\n                                   Contract.\\n                        1.1.6.3     “Plant” means the machinery, equipment, and apparatus and the likes, intended\\n                                      to form or forming part of the Permanent Works, including the supply-only items (if\\n                                    any), which are to be supplied by the Contractor as specified in the Contract.\\n                        1.1.6.4     “Section/Station” means a part of the Works specifically designated in the Scope\\n                                      of Work as a Section/Station (if any).\\n                        1.1.6.5     “Site” means the places provided by the Employer where the Works are to be\\n                               executed and to which Plant and Materials are to be delivered and any other place\\n                              as may be specifically designated in the Contract as forming part of the Site.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"1.1.6.6     “Variation” means any  alteration  and/  or  modification  to  the  Employer’s\\n                         Requirements& Technical specifications, which is instructed by the Engineer or\\n                          approved as a variation by the Employer, in accordance with Clause 12.\\n                   1.1.6.7    “Works” means the work to be executed in accordance with the Contract and shall\\n                              include both Permanent Works and Temporary Works.\\n                   1.1.6.8    “Permanent Works” means the permanent works to be executed, completed and\\n                            maintained in accordance with the works and the remedying of any defect\\n                                therein, and which subsequently be removed by the Contractor.\\n                  1.1.6.10    “Project” means Bangalore Metro Rail Project.\\n\\nInterpretation     1.2      In the Contract except where the context requires otherwise:\\n\\n                   1.2.1   (a) words indicating one gender include all genders;\\n                              (b) words indicating the singular also include the plural and words indicating the plural\\n                              also include the singular and\\n                               (c) “Written” or “in writing” means hand-written, type written, printed or electronically\\n                      made and resulting in a permanent record.\\n\\n                     The marginal words and other headings shall not be taken into consideration in the\\n                             interpretation of these conditions\\n                          Various clauses of GCC are to be read with relevant ‘Contract data’ appended to the\\n                GCC and are to be interpreted accordingly.\\n\\n                   1.2.2\\n                     Terms and expressions not herein defined shall have the meaning assigned to them in\\n                          the “Indian General Clauses Act 1897” or the Indian Contract Act or the Indian Sale\\n                     Goods Act or any other applicable Indian Law, as case may be.\\n\\n\\nLaw and          1.3    The Contract shall be governed by the Acts and Laws of India, the rules, regulations\\nLanguage             and bye-laws of the concerned public bodies and authorities.\\n                      Language of the Contract shall be English.\\n\\nContract          1.4    The Employer and the Contractor shall execute a Contract Agreement in the form\\nAgreement             annexed to the Contract Forms, incorporating any necessary modifications, within the\\n                        timeframe specified in the Contract Data. The Contractor shall be responsible for and\\n                        bear all costs associated with stamp duties, registration fees, and other applicable\\n                             levies imposed by law.\\n                                            If the Contractor is a JV, the authorized representative of each member of the JV shall\\n                          sign the Contract agreement.\\n\\n                         Modification to contract to be in writing:\\n\\n                             In the event of any of the provisions of the contract requiring to be modified after the\\n                           contract documents have been signed, the modifications shall be made in writing and\\n                         signed by the Employer and the Contractor, and no work shall proceed under such\\n                           modifications until this has been done. Any verbal or written arrangements abandoning,\\n                           modifying, extending, reducing or supplementing the contract or any of the terms\\n                           thereof shall be deemed conditional and shall not be binding on the Employer unless\\n                      and until the same is incorporated in a formal instrument and signed by the Employer\\n                      and the Contractor, and  till then the Employer shall have the right to repudiate such\\n                        arrangements.\\n\\nPriority of        1.5    The documents forming the Contract are to be taken as mutually explanatory of one\\nDocuments               another.  If there is any conflict, ambiguity or discrepancy in the documents, the\\n                        Engineer shall issue any necessary clarification or instruction to the Contractor, and\\n                          the priority of the documents shall be as follows,\\n                                 1.  Contract Agreement.\\n                                 2.  Letter of Acceptance.\\n                                 3.  Clarifications, Addenda, Corrigenda Issued to Tender document (if any)\\n                                 4.  Pricing Document\\n                                 5.  Special Conditions of Contract\\n                                 6.  Contract forms.\\n                                 7.  General Conditions of Contract\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 74,\n      \"text\": \"8.  Employer’s Requirement &Technical Specifications\\n                                       9. Drawings\\n                                   10. Any other document forming part of the Contract\\n\\n    Care              1.6    The specification and drawings shall be in the custody and care of the Employer.\\n    and Supply of           Unless otherwise stated in the contract, one copy of the contract and two copies of\\n    Documents            each subsequent Drawing shall be supplied to the contractor, who may make or\\n                              request further copies at the cost of the contractor.\\n                         The Construction and/or Manufacture Documents shall be in the custody and care of\\n                               the Contractor during the Contract.  Unless otherwise stated  in the Employer’s\\n                             Requirements, the Contractor shall provide six copies (physical) and one  digital copy\\n                                     for the use of the Engineer and assistants.\\n                         The Contractor shall keep on Site one complete set of the documents forming the\\n                                Contract,  the  Construction  and/or  Manufacture  Documents,  Variations,  other\\n                            communications given or issued from time to time and the documents/samples\\n                            mentioned in Sub-Clause 5.3. The Employer, the Engineer and their assistants shall\\n                           have the right to access these documents all reasonable times.\\n                      On discovery of any technical error or defect in a document intended to be used for the\\n                            purpose of Contract, the Contractor shall promptly give notice to the Engineer of such\\n                                  error or defect.\\n\\n    Communications 1.7      All communications between the parties, unless otherwise specified, shall be valid and\\n                                   effective only when made in writing. A notice shall be deemed effective only when sent\\n                                   to the party's designated address via speed post, courier, email to the specified email\\n                                  ID, or delivered by hand.\\n\\n    Employer's       1.8    As between the Parties, the Contractor shall retain the copyright and other intellectual\\n    Use of                    property rights over the Contractor's Documents and other design documents made by\\n     Contractor's                                   (or on behalf of) the Contractor.\\n    Documents\\n\\n                         The Contractor shall be deemed (by signing the Contract) to give to the Employer a\\n                              non-terminable transferable non-exclusive  royalty-free licence  to copy, use and\\n                           communicate the Contractor's Documents, including making and using modifications\\n                                   of them. This licence shall:\\n                                     (a) apply throughout the actual or intended working life (whichever is longer) of the\\n                                     relevant parts of the Works,\\n                                     (b) entitle any person in proper possession of the relevant part of the Works to copy,\\n                               use and communicate  the  Contractor's Documents  for  the  purposes  of\\n                                    completing, operating, maintaining, altering, adjusting, repairing and demolishing\\n                                    the Works, and\\n                                       (c) in the case of Contractor's Documents which are in the form of computer programs\\n                              and other software, permit their use on any computer on the Site and other places\\n                                as envisaged by the Contract, including replacements of any computers supplied\\n                               by the Contractor.\\n                                        After payment of the consideration under the Contract to the Contractor all the\\n                                          intellectual property rights of Contractor vested in the Works/services, executed\\n                                 under the Contract,  will get transferred and vested with the Employer. The\\n                                     contractor shall take necessary action in this regard.\\n\\n     Contractor's      1.9    As between the Parties, the Employer shall retain the copyright and other intellectual\\n    Use of                    property rights in the Employer's Requirements & Technical specifications and other\\n    Employer's            documents made by (or on behalf of) the Employer. The Contractor may, at his cost,\\n    Documents              copy, use, and obtain communication of these documents for the purposes of the\\n                                Contract.\\n                          They shall not, without the Employer's consent, be copied, used or communicated to a\\n                                     third party by the Contractor, except as necessary for the purposes of the Contract.\\n    Compliance      1.10   The Contractor shall familiarise themselves and conform to the requirement in all\\n     with Statutes,           aspects with:\\n     Regulations                (a) the provision of any enactment in India as applicable from time to time\\n    and Laws                   (b) the regulations or bye-laws of any local body and utilities including, but not limited\\n                                       to Electricity Supply Regulation Act, Indian Electricity Rules, Pollution Control\\n                                   Rules, BBMP(GBA) rules and regulations and byelaws etc.\\n                                     (c) The Contractor shall be bound to give all notices required by statute, regulations or\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"bye-laws, as aforesaid and to pay all fees and bills payable in respect thereof. The\\n                              Contractor will arrange necessary clearances and approvals before the Work is\\n                            taken up.\\n                        Ignorance of Rules, Regulations and Bye-laws shall not constitute a basis for any claim\\n                             at any stage of work. The Contractor shall indemnify the Employer against all penalties\\n                      and liabilities of every kind of breach of any such enactment, laws, regulations, bye-\\n                        laws or rules.\\nJoint and        1.11      If the Contractor is (under applicable Laws) a joint venture or consortium of two or more\\nSeveral                    Parties:\\nLiability\\n                           a)  these parties shall be deemed to be jointly and severally liable to the Employer for\\n                              the performance of the Contract;\\n                           b)  these parties shall notify the Employer of their lead member who shall have\\n                                authority to bind the Contractor and each of these parties; and\\n                             c)  the Contractor shall not alter its composition or legal status without the prior consent\\n                                  of the Employer.\\n\\nSeverability      1.12      If any clause, provision, section or part of the Contract is ruled invalid by a court of\\n                       competent jurisdiction, then the parties shall: (a) promptly meet and negotiate a\\n                            substitute for such clause, provision, section or part, which shall, to the greatest extent\\n                              legally permissible, effect the original intent of the parties, and (b)  if necessary or\\n                            desirable, apply to the court which declared such invalidity for a judicial construction of\\n                          the invalidated portion to guide the negotiations. The invalidity or enforceability of any\\n                       such clause, provision, section or part shall not affect the validity or unenforceability of\\n                          the balance of the Contract, which shall be construed and enforced as if the Contract\\n                           did not contain such invalid or unenforceable clause, provision, section or part.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"1      DEFINITIONS AND INTERPRETATION\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Definitions and Interpretation\"\n}",

            "quote": "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"2\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"2     The Employer\\n\\n\\nGeneral          2.1    Employer shall give the Contractor right of access to, and possession of, all parts of\\nObligations              the Site within the time (or times) stated in the Contract Data and shall pay the\\n                           contractor in accordance with the contract.\\nAccess to and    2.2        If no such time is stated in the Contract Data, the Employer shall grant the Contractor\\nPossession of              right of access to, and possession of, the Site progressively. The Contractor  will\\nthe Site                 draw/modify  the  schedule  for  completion  of Works  according  to  progressive\\n                           possession/right of such sites. The right and possession may not be exclusive to the\\n                           Contractor.\\n\\n                                            If the Contractor suffers delay as a result of a failure by the Employer to grant right of\\n                       access to, or possession within such time, the Contractor shall give notice to the\\n                        Engineer within a period of 28 days of such occurrence. After receipt of such notice,\\n                          the Engineer shall proceed to determine any extension of time to which the Contractor\\n                                  is entitled and shall notify the Contractor accordingly.\\n                        For any such delay in handing over of  site, Contractors  will be entitled to only\\n                        reasonable extension of time and no monetary claims whatsoever shall be paid or\\n                           entertained on this account.\\n\\nPermits,          2.3       It shall be Contractor’s exclusive responsibility to get approvals, permits or license\\nLicences or              required for the Contract. However, the Employer may (where he is in a position to do\\nApprovals               so) provide reasonable assistance to Contractor at the request and cost of the\\n                          Contractor in getting Permits, License or Approvals required during the Contract.\\n                     The rendering of such assistance by the Employer shall not be interpreted as a pretext\\n                       by the Contractor as condoning of any delay or non-performance of any of the\\n                          Contractors  obligations. The  following-up  of  all such  applications  shall be  the\\n                             responsibility of the Contractor.\\n\\nAssignment by   2.4    The Employer shall be fully entitled without the consent of the Contractor, to assign the\\nthe Employer             benefit of the Contract or any part thereof and any interest therein or there under to\\n                      any third party.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"2     The Employer\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"The Employer\"\n}",

            "quote": "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"3\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"3     The Engineer\\n\\nAppointment of  3.1    The Employer shall notify the Contractor in writing of the appointment and identity of\\nEngineer                 the Engineer and of any replacement from time to time.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 76,\n      \"text\": \"Duties and       3.2    The Engineer shall carry out the duties specified in the Contract. The Engineer shall\\n     Authorities of          have no authority to amend the Contract.\\n     the Engineer          The Engineer may exercise the authority specified in, or necessarily to be implied from\\n                               the Contract. Any requisite approval shall be deemed to have been given by the\\n                           Employer for any such authority exercised by the Engineer.\\n\\n                         The Engineer shall have no authority to relieve the Contractor of any of his duties,\\n                                  obligations, or responsibilities under the Contract. Engineer shall watch and inspect the\\n                            Works, monitor the test results and examine any material to be used and workmanship\\n                           employed by the Contractor in connection with the Works. Any proposal, inspection,\\n                               examination, testing, consent, approval or similar act by the Engineer (including\\n                           absence of disapproval) shall not relieve the Contractor from any responsibility,\\n                                 including responsibility for his errors, omissions, discrepancies, and non-compliance\\n                                 with Sub-Clause 5.4.\\n\\n                         The Engineer shall copy to the Employer all communications given or received by him\\n                                     in accordance with the Contract.\\n\\n     Engineer’s       3.3         i. The Engineer, with the prior approval of the Employer may from time to time assign\\n     Authority to            and delegate authority to Engineer’s representatives/assistants and may also revoke\\n     Delegate               such assignments and delegations. The delegation or revocation shall be in writing and\\n                                   shall be applicable only after same has been notified in writing to the Contractor.\\n\\n                                                             ii. Each Engineer’s representative/assistants to whom duties have been assigned or\\n                                  authority has been delegated shall be authorized to issue instructions to the Contractor\\n                                   to the extent defined by the delegation. Any determination, approval, check, certificate,\\n                               consent, examination, inspection, instruction, notice, proposal, request, test or similar\\n                                act by an Engineer’s representative/assistants shall have the same effect as though\\n                               the act had been an act of the Engineer. However:\\n                                   (a)   any failure to disapprove any Plant, Material, design and workmanship shall not\\n                                      prejudice the right of the Engineer to reject such Plant, Material, Design and\\n                                  workmanship;\\n                                   (b)       if the Contractor questions any determination or  instruction  of Engineer’s\\n                                        representative/assistants, the Contractor may refer the matter to the Engineer\\n                                         within three days of such decision having been given, who shall confirm, reverse\\n                                       or vary such determination or instruction.\\n\\n     Engineer’s       3.4    Engineer may issue to the Contractor instructions  (at any time) which may be\\n     Instructions             necessary for the execution of the works. The Contractor shall comply with instructions\\n                              given by the Engineer.\\n                         The Contractor shall only take instruction from the Engineer, or from the Engineer’s\\n                              Representative or an Assistant to whom the appropriate authority to give instruction\\n                           has been delegated under Sub-clause 3.3.\\n\\n                       No act or omission by the Engineer or Engineer’s representative/assistants in the\\n                            performance of any of the Engineer’s duties or the exercise of any of the Engineer’s\\n                           powers under the Contract shall, in any way, operate to relieve the Contractor of any\\n                                   of the duties, responsibilities, obligations or liabilities imposed upon the Contractor by\\n                           any of the provisions of the Contract.\\n\\n   Agreement or     3.5   When the Engineer is required to determine value, cost or extension of time, he shall\\n    Determination             consult with the Contractor and the Employer in an endeavour to reach agreement.  If\\n                           agreement is not achieved, the Engineer shall determine the matter fairly, reasonably\\n                          and in accordance with the Contract, with the approval of Employer.\\n    Meetings called   3.6    The Engineer may  require  the  Contractor  and/or  its  representative  to  attend\\n   by the Engineer        management meetings to discuss arrangements for future work or other matters\\n                                 related to the execution of the Works.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"3     The Engineer\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"The Engineer\"\n}",

            "quote": "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"4.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"4.0      Contractor SHE Policy and Plan\\n\\n          4.1     The contractor as per Section 39 of the BOCW Act shall formulate a SHE policy and\\n                    get it approved by DG respectively and display it at conspicuous places at work sites\\n                        in Kannada, Hindi and other languages understood by the majority of construction\\n                   workers.\\n\\n\\n          4.2       Within 4 weeks of the notification of acceptance of the tender, the Contractor shall\\n                  submit a detailed and comprehensive Contract specific SHE Plan. The SHE Plan shall\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"include detailed policies, procedures and regulations which, when implemented, will\\n         ensure compliance  of the contract  provisions. The SHE Plan  shall include the\\n          following but not be restricted to:\\n\\n\\n                i)   A statement of the Contractor’s policy, organisation and arrangements for SHE\\n\\n                  ii)   The name(s) and experience of person(s) within the Contractor’s proposed\\n            management who shall be responsible for co-ordinating and monitoring the\\n                Contractor’s SHE performance;\\n\\n                  iii)   The number  of SHE  staff who  shall be employed on  the Works,  their\\n                  responsibilities,  authority  and  line  of  communication  with  the  proposed\\n                Contractor's agent;\\n\\n             iv)   A statement  of the Contractor’s policy and procedures  for  identifying and\\n                estimating hazards, and the measures for addressing the same;\\n\\n           v)   A list of SHE hazards anticipated for this Contract and sufficient information to\\n              demonstrate the Contractor’s proposals for achieving effective and  efficient\\n                health and safety procedures;\\n\\n             vi)   A description of the SHE training courses and emergency drills which shall\\n\\n             be provided by the Contractor, with an outline of the syllabus to be followed;\\n\\n              vii)   Details of the safety equipment which shall be provided by the Contractor,\\n                including personal protective equipment;\\n\\n               viii)  A statement of the Contractor’s policy and procedures for ensuring that\\n                Contractor's Equipment used on the Project Site are maintained in a safe\\n                condition and are operated in a safe manner;\\n\\n             ix)  A statement of the Contractor’s policy and procedures for ensuring that sub-\\n                contractors comply with the Contractor's safety plan;\\n\\n           x)   A statement of the Contractor’s disciplinary procedures with respect to SHE\\n                 related matters, and\\n\\n             xi)  A statement of the Contractor’s procedure for reporting and investigating\\n                accidents, dangerous occurrences or occupational illnesses\\n\\n\\n4.3     The Contractor shall, from time to time and as necessary are required by the\\n         Employer to produce supplements to the SHE Plan such that  it is at all times a\\n           detailed, comprehensive and contemporaneous statement by the Contractor of his\\n            site safety, industrial health and environment obligations, responsibilities, policies\\n        and procedures relating to work on Site. Any and all submissions of supplements\\n           to the SHE Plan shall be made to the Employer in accordance with the agreed\\n         procedures.\\n\\n4.4           If at any time the SHE plan is, in the Employer’s opinion, insufficient or requires\\n           revision or modification to ensure the security of the Works and the safety of all\\n        workmen upon and visitors to the Site, the Employer may instruct the Contractor\\n           to revise the SHE plan and the Contractor shall within 7 days submit the revised\\n          plan to the Employer for review.\\n\\n\\n4.5     Any omissions, inconsistencies and errors in the SHE Plan or the Employer’s\\n         acceptance or rejection of the SHE Plan and/or supplements thereto shall be\\n          without prejudice  to the Contractor's  obligations with respect  to  site  safety,\\n           industrial health and environment and  shall not excuse any  failure by the\\n          contractor  to adopt proper and recognised  safety  practices throughout  the\\n          execution of the Work.\\n\\n\\n4.6     The Contractor  shall adhere  to the SHE Plan and  shall ensure, as  far as\\n           practically possible, that  all sub-contractors of  all tiers require that contracting\\n           parties each have a copy of the Site SHE Plan and comply with its provisions.\\n\\n\\n4.7     The details of contents to be covered in the site SHE plan are given in Appendix\\n         No.: 3.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"          4.0      Contractor SHE Policy and Plan\",\n  \"retained_lines\":
